| [F-QOS-041](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-041) | Per-rank quiesce agent | redesign | The per-rank agent that turns sets into local quiesce actions is internal (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-042](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-042) | Quiesce on degraded file systems and non-active ranks | redesign | C++ cancels every set while any rank recovers; per-shard recovery could keep unaffected shards quiesced (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-043](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-043) | Tree quiesce request (quiesce_path) | redesign | With hierarchical S/X/IS/IX locks, quiescing a root is one subtree-level lock that conflicts with writers' intention locks, not an internal request walk per path (D12, D16). |  |
| [F-QOS-044](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-044) | Per-inode quiesce request (quiesce_inode) | redesign | Outcome: write and exclusive caps under the root are revoked and acked before QUIESCED; the per-inode request walk is C++ mechanism (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-045](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-045) | quiescelock and parking of mutations | redesign | Outcome: mutations under a quiesced root park while reads, mksnap, flushes and file locks proceed; quiescelock itself is C++ internal (D12, D16). |  |
| [F-QOS-046](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-046) | Cap masking while quiesced | contract | Clients see only p As Ls Xs Fs Fr Fc Fb while quiesced (keeping Fb avoids forced write-back), and QA asserts it (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-049](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-049) | Quiesce and subtree migration | redesign | A shard migration (D6) must not let a subtree escape an active quiesce: refuse it or carry the quiesce state (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-050](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-050) | Quiesce release and cancel cascade | redesign | Release drops the quiesce, wakes parked requests and re-issues caps; mechanism free (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-052](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-052) | ceph.quiesce.block vxattr | contract | Persistent ceph.quiesce.block flag exempting a subtree, set by clients through the ceph.* xattr passthrough (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-053](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-053) | quiesce db command | contract | quiesce db is what mgr/volumes fs quiesce sends to the qdb leader; its JSON layout and errnos are documented API (D12, F-ADM-054). | Until built, answer with a plain error (e.g. EOPNOTSUPP), never -ENOTTY naming this daemon as leader: the mgr would retry 5 times.<br>Question: ceph-csi's CephFS VolumeGroupSnapshot (outside the map) is believed to call fs quiesce; verify before keeping quiesce at P2. |
| [F-QOS-057](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-057) | Quiesce observability: counters and op events | redesign | Quiesce progress visibility (ops in flight, counters) for operators and QA; the C++ names follow its per-inode ops (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-058](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-058) | quarantine enable and disable commands | contract | Quarantine is P2 (D12); mgr/volumes fs subvolume quarantine sends these commands and returns the JSON verbatim (F-ADM-055). | Reach the owning rank instead of answering -EXDEV: the mgr picks the first active MDS and its comment expects forwarding. |
| [F-QOS-060](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-060) | Persistent quarantine flag in inode optmetadata | redesign | The flag must persist with the subvolume root and survive failover; its on-disk encoding is free (D2, D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-061](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-061) | In-progress quarantine state and operation ordering | redesign | Enforcement from acceptance and its ordering with the journal commit are internal (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-062](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-062) | Per-subvolume quarantine operation registry | redesign | One enable/disable per subvolume at a time with EBUSY (QA retries on EBUSY); mechanism free (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-063](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-063) | Quarantine undo on failure | redesign | Undo of the in-progress state on failure is internal (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-064](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-064) | Quarantine cap-revocation walk | redesign | Caps must drop to PIN (or come back) at once; with shard = subvolume all its caps are on the owning rank, removing the C++ coverage gap across subtree bounds (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-065](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-065) | Quarantine propagation to replica ranks | redesign | Only the parent shard's boundary copy of the subvolume root needs the flag (D5); no general replica propagation (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-067](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-067) | Quarantine re-enforcement after restart | redesign | After failover, reconnected caps on a quarantined subvolume must again be limited to PIN (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-068](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-068) | Quarantine enforcement predicate | redesign | With shard = subvolume, 'under quarantine' is a flag of the inode's shard (O(1), can fail closed) instead of a realm-chain walk on every request and cap issue (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-069](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-069) | MDS auth cap flags q and Q (quarantine access) | contract | Quarantine-specific use of the q/Q grant flags in access checks and cap issue (D12; matches F-ADM-015). | Risk: The MDS cap parser must already accept q and Q in P0 (F-ADM-006/015): the mon accepts such grants and clients carrying them must still mount. |
| [F-QOS-070](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-070) | Quarantine client feature bit 24 | contract | The client's bit 24 selects EACCES (aware) or parking (unaware) once quarantine exists (D12). | P0 does not advertise bit 24 (D15); libcephfs never tests the MDS's bit 24. |
| [F-QOS-071](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-071) | EACCES for quarantine-aware sessions | contract | mgr/volumes infers quarantine from EACCES (subvolume info) and aware libcephfs clients expect it (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-072](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-072) | Request parking for quarantine-unaware sessions | contract | Quarantine-unaware clients (every kernel) block until the quarantine is lifted (D12). | Park before taking locks: C++ parked requests keep path locks and can wedge enable and disable. |
| [F-QOS-073](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-073) | Snapshot operation parking under quarantine | contract | Snapshot operations of unaware clients on a quarantined subvolume park, protecting recovery snapshots (D12; snapshots P1). |  |
| [F-QOS-074](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-074) | Cap restriction to PIN under quarantine | contract | Clients without q/Q get only PIN on quarantined inodes (D12). |  |
| [F-QOS-075](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-075) | EQUARANTINED errno in MClientCaps | contract | libcephfs marks an inode quarantined on a revoke carrying -EQUARANTINED (D12). | Risk: Whenever MClientCaps v14 is sent before quarantine exists, oserrno must be 0. |
| [F-QOS-076](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-076) | QUARANTINE marker in InodeStat | contract | libcephfs detects quarantined subtrees from optmetadata kind 2 on the subvolume root (D12). | Risk: Never emit optmetadata kind 2 before quarantine exists. |
| [F-QOS-077](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-077) | MQuarantineDisable notification | contract | Quarantine-aware clients clear their mark on MQuarantineDisable (D12). | Risk: Send it uncounted (push_seq counts only CAPS, SNAP, LEASE, QUOTA): C++ counts it while libcephfs does not, so a later REQUEST_CLOSE is dropped. Never send it to the kernel. |
| [F-ADM-054](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-054) | quiesce db command | contract | Quiesce is P2 (D12); mgr/volumes `fs quiesce` sends quiesce db to mdsmap.qdb_leader and follows -ENOTTY {leader}. | Risk: Until then `fs quiesce` fails; no default cephadm or Rook flow uses it. |
| [F-ADM-055](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-055) | quarantine enable / disable command | contract | Quarantine is P2 (D12); mgr/volumes sends `quarantine enable\|disable path=` to the first active rank. | Risk: The rank mgr/volumes picks (normally rank 0) must reach the shard owner instead of returning -EXDEV as C++ does. |
| [F-ADM-101](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-101) | mgr/volumes: fs quiesce | contract | Quiesce is P2 (D12). |  |
| [F-ADM-102](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-102) | mgr/volumes: subvolume quarantine | contract | Quarantine is P2 (D12). |  |

### Metrics, QoS, quiesce and quarantine: OUT (9)

| Entry | Name | Why |
|---|---|---|
| [F-QOS-001](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-001) | QoS admission gate for client metadata requests | dmClock admission is superseded by the internal per-shard fair scheduler (D11); it has no wire contract (latency only, off by default). |
| [F-QOS-002](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-002) | QoS bucket classification from the session mount root | Buckets keyed by the client-asserted mount root are replaced by the shard an inode belongs to (D3, D11). |
| [F-QOS-003](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-003) | QoS per-op cost table | A compiled-in dmClock cost table (1 for 11 read ops, 3 otherwise); the per-shard scheduler defines its own cost model (D11). |
| [F-QOS-004](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-004) | dmClock tag-based scheduling per bucket | Tag scheduling per mount-root bucket is dmClock-specific behaviour (D11) and gives no capacity-aware SLA anyway (qos-dmclock-scheduler 8). |
| [F-QOS-006](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-006) | Default QoS parameters (mds_dmclock_* options) | dmClock admin surface (D11); no mgr module, cephadm or Rook sets mds_dmclock_*, and only the MDS reads them (matches F-ADM-056). |
| [F-QOS-007](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-007) | Per-bucket QoS overrides (qos set, qos rm) | Per-rank, in-memory overrides keyed by mount-root prefix are dmClock admin surface (D11) with no programmatic caller (matches F-ADM-056). |
| [F-QOS-008](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-008) | QoS inspection commands and per-bucket statistics | dump qos / qos get inspect dmClock buckets (D11); no peer parses them (matches F-ADM-056). |
| [F-QOS-066](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-066) | Subtree export refused inside a quarantined subvolume | Whole-shard migration (D6) carries the persistent flag; refusing exports would block max_mds reduction, which C++ QA has to work around. |
| [F-ADM-056](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-056) | QoS commands (dump qos, qos set, qos rm, qos get) | dmClock admin surface (D11), superseded by per-shard fair scheduling; no mgr module or orchestrator consumes it. |

<a id="snapshots"></a>

## Snapshots

### Snapshots: P0 (13)

| Entry | Name | Mode | Why | Scope, risk and open question |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [F-CLU-092](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-092) | Snaprealm reconciliation and snapflush rebuild at the end of rejoin | subset | P0 keeps snap realms for quota and subvolume roots (D8); clients report their realms in reconnect and must get MClientSnap UPDATE/SPLIT when their view is stale, or client-side quota enforcement uses the wrong realm. | Realm reconciliation (UPDATE/SPLIT and the end-of-rejoin update) for quota/subvolume realms; snapflush rebuild (LOCK_SNAP_SYNC) comes with P1 snapshots.<br>Risk: A malformed snap trace makes the kernel blocklist itself; a SPLIT needs split_inos/split_realms. |
| [F-PROTO-061](../mds-rust-map/features/02-client-sessions-and-protocol.md#f-proto-061) | Snap realm reconnect | subset | Snap realms exist in P0 for the root, quota roots and subvolume roots (D8) and clients report their realms in reconnect; a client with an older realm seq must get an MClientSnap UPDATE. | Accept realm records from v3 (raw) and v5 reconnects and send the catch-up UPDATE; no snapshot state in P0. |
| [F-PROTO-085](../mds-rust-map/features/02-client-sessions-and-protocol.md#f-proto-085) | Snap trace encoding and format selection | contract | Snap traces accompany every reply, cap IMPORT and MClientSnap even without snapshots (D8); the kernel never sets bit 19 and needs SnapRealmInfo, while libcephfs decodes SnapRealmInfoNew when the MDS advertises bit 19. | Advertise bit 19; old format for sessions without client bit 19, new format (last_modified, change_attr, flags) for those with it; P0 realms carry no snaps.<br>Risk: A snap trace the kernel cannot parse makes it blocklist itself. |
| [F-PROTO-094](../mds-rust-map/features/02-client-sessions-and-protocol.md#f-proto-094) | MClientSnap encoding | subset | Without snapshots (D8) P0 still creates realms for quota and subvolume roots, tells clients with SPLIT and catches up reconnecting clients with UPDATE. | SPLIT (split inos and realms, trace starting with the split realm) for realm creation and moves, UPDATE for reconnect catch-up; mksnap/rmsnap and global-realm notifications are P1. Counted in push_seq.<br>Risk: libcephfs asserts that nothing follows the trace. |
| [F-SNAP-006](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-006) | Snap realms and effective snapshot sets | subset | D8: realms must exist for /, quota roots and subvolume (shard) roots because clients find quota roots by walking realms; the persisted realm format must already hold everything P1 snapshots need. | P0: realms for /, quota directories and shard roots with an empty effective snapshot set; persisted realm node with seq, created, last_created, last_destroyed, current_parent_since, snaps, past_parent_snaps, flags (PARENT_GLOBAL, SUBVOLUME, SNAPDIR_VISIBILITY), last_modified, change_attr. Snapshot, hard-link and past-parent realms P1; legacy past_parents and old sr_t versions OUT. |
| [F-SNAP-007](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-007) | Realm membership of caps and realm split on creation | contract | Creating a quota or subvolume realm on a directory in use (resize of an unsized subvolume, group quota) must tell each client exactly which cached inodes and child realms moved (SPLIT lists); cap grants name the containing realm. | Per-shard realm membership tracking replaces the walk of inodes_with_caps under mds_lock.<br>Risk: A cap grant naming a realm not yet sent in a trace makes the kclient WARN; readdir must not give caps on entries in another realm. |
| [F-SNAP-008](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-008) | Snap trace contents and client interpretation | contract | D8: every reply and cap IMPORT carries the snap trace from the target realm to the root; the kclient always gets the old format (it never sets bit 19), libcephfs the new one if the MDS advertises bit 19. | Risk: A malformed trace or a wrong trace_len makes the kclient blocklist itself.<br>Question: D15: advertise NEW_SNAPREALM_INFO (bit 19) in P0? Without it libcephfs decodes old-format traces and loses SNAPDIR_VISIBILITY and the snapdir mtime/change_attr. |
| [F-SNAP-009](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-009) | Per-realm MClientSnap notification (always op SPLIT) | contract | Quota and subvolume realm creation or change is announced with MClientSnap op SPLIT (lists filled only for a new realm), sent counted on the session before the request's reply. | Risk: libcephfs asserts split != 0 and that the first trace element is the split realm; the messages count toward push_seq, which REQUEST_CLOSE checks. |
| [F-SNAP-013](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-013) | Create a snapshot (MKSNAP, mkdir in .snap) | tolerate | D8: P0 refuses snapshot creation as when allow_new_snaps is off; full MKSNAP (mgr/volumes, snap_schedule, ceph-csi VolumeSnapshots, cephfs-mirror) is P1. | P0: MKSNAP -> EPERM (EROFS on a read-only MDS). P1: the full op (SnapPayload, check order, realm creation, notifications).<br>Risk: mgr/volumes snapshot create and ceph-csi snapshots and clones fail until P1.<br>Question: Snapshots of directories above shard roots (/, /volumes, groups; snap_schedule on / is common) span many shards: support them in P1 with cross-shard coordination or refuse them? Decide before freezing the P0 format. |
| [F-SNAP-021](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-021) | Snapshot path encoding, traversal and LOOKUPSNAP | subset | Clients send snapdir paths in P0: the kernel splices .snap on ENOENT, mgr/volumes stats .snap/<name> before mkdir and lists .snap on subvolume rm; they must get ENOENT or empty answers, not errors. | P0: parse the empty first path component; LOOKUPSNAP and lookups under .snap -> ENOENT; mutations under .snap -> EROFS; never create dentries for snapshot paths. Snapshot name resolution (own names, _<name>_<ino>) and snapid traversal P1. |
| [F-SNAP-022](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-022) | List snapshots (LSSNAP, readdir of .snap) | tolerate | D8: P0 serves an empty LSSNAP; mgr/volumes (subvolume rm, snapshot ls), the Rook cleanup job and ls of .snap list the snapdir. | Empty listing with FRAG_END\|FRAG_COMPLETE, DirStat and the directory realm's trace; full listing (plain own names, _<name>_<ino> for inherited ones, path2 paging) P1. |
| [F-SNAP-031](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-031) | Realm state replication between ranks (MMDSSnapUpdate, snaplock payload) | redesign | Shards on other ranks must learn realm changes above their roots (a group quota realm becomes the parent of the subvolume realms below it) and notify their clients; MMDSSnapUpdate and snaplock payloads are C++ mechanisms (D16). | P0: propagate realm creation and re-parenting above shard roots to the owning shards and their clients; P1: snapshot-set changes to every shard. |
| [F-SNAP-032](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-032) | Snaprealm reconciliation at client reconnect and rejoin | subset | D9 failover: clients report realms and per-cap realms in MClientReconnect; the MDS must correct clients whose realm view is stale (for example a quota realm created just before the failure). | P0: accept the realm records and send traces/SPLIT for caps reconnected under an outdated realm; snapflush rebuild P1.<br>Risk: Never emit SPLIT with split = 0 (possible in C++; libcephfs asserts). |

### Snapshots: P1 (44)

| Entry | Name | Mode | Why | Scope, risk and open question |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [F-CLU-013](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-013) | Mirroring metadata (MirrorInfo) in the FSMap | drop | MirrorInfo lives in the FSMap, is edited by mon commands and read by cephfs-mirror; the MDS never reads it, and mirroring itself comes with P1 snapshots (D8). |  |
| [F-CLU-078](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-078) | Snap table recovery and the SERVER_READY gate | redesign | P0 has no snap table (D8: mksnap returns EPERM), so the SERVER_READY gate and the rank-0 dependency vanish; P1 snapshots need a file-system-wide snapid allocator whose recovery must not hold other ranks in up:reconnect. |  |
| [F-CAPS-023](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-023) | Snapshot COW of cap state and SNAP_SYNC gating | redesign | Snapshots are P1 (D8); P0 refuses MKSNAP, so no written inode is ever COWed. Design the cap side together with the snapshot redesign. |  |
| [F-CAPS-024](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-024) | FLUSHSNAP / FLUSHSNAP_ACK protocol | contract | Comes with snapshots (D8): clients send FLUSHSNAP only for capsnaps, which need a new snapshot in their realm. | Risk: If one arrives in P0, ack it from a rank where the client holds a cap, echoing snap_follows and tid (libcephfs asserts otherwise). |
| [F-CAPS-025](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-025) | Null snapflush inference | redesign | Only needed while snapflushes are pending (snapshots, D8); P0 ignores CEPH_CLIENT_CAPS_PENDING_CAPSNAP. |  |
| [F-CAPS-026](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-026) | Snapflush nudge | redesign | Forcing slow snapflushes only matters once snapshots exist (D8). |  |
| [F-CAPS-027](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-027) | XLOCKSNAP: truncate with pending snapflush | redesign | Truncation must wait for buffered data owed to a pending snapshot; only relevant with snapshots (D8). The XLOCKSNAP state itself is internal (D16). |  |
| [F-CAPS-045](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-045) | Infinite leases on snapshot listings | contract | LSSNAP returns no entries in P0 (D8), so no snapshot lease record is ever sent; comes with snapshots. |  |
| [F-NS-029](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-029) | Reads at a snapshot id (LOOKUPSNAP, snapshot LOOKUPINO, open and readdir in snapshots) | contract | Reads at a snapshot id exist only with snapshots (D8); in P0 .snap paths are handled by F-SNAP-021/022 and writes by the EROFS gate (F-NS-017). |  |
| [F-NS-070](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-070) | ceph.mirror.* xattrs for snapshot mirroring | contract | Only snapshot mirroring (mgr mirroring peer_add, cephfs-mirror dirty_snap_id) uses them; mirroring is P1 (D8). | Storing them as plain xattrs is trivial and may land early. |
| [F-CACHE-011](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-011) | Snapshot-range identity of inodes and dentries | redesign | Snapshots are P1 (D8): dentries and inodes must then exist in several snapshot-era versions ([first,last]). | Design the P0 format so dentry versions keyed by (name, last snapid) and per-inode old versions can be added. |
| [F-CACHE-072](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-072) | Global snaprealm dummy inode | redesign | The global realm (ino 3) gives hard-linked inodes outside subvolumes their snapshot semantics, so it comes with snapshots (D8); clients merely keep realm 3 when they receive it. | Keep number 3 reserved. |
| [F-STORE-024](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-024) | Snapshot COW images in the journal | redesign | Copy-on-write of dentries and inodes at snapshot boundaries comes with snapshots (D8); the WAL record format must leave room for it. |  |
| [F-STORE-077](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-077) | SnapTable object (mds_snaptable) | redesign | Snapshot ids must be unique and monotonic per file system and start above the data pools' existing snap seqs; this comes with snapshots (D8). | Legacy format upgrade is out. |
| [F-SNAP-001](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-001) | Snapshot id and realm seq allocation from one monotonic counter | redesign | Snapshot ids must be unique and increasing file-system-wide, above every data pool's snap_seq (OSD self-managed snaps), and share the sequence with realm seq that clients compare (D8: P1). | Design now: an FS-wide snapid/seq allocator usable by every shard (data pools are shared across shards); P0 realm seqs stay constant. |
| [F-SNAP-002](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-002) | Rank-0 snap table service (SnapServer) and per-rank SnapClient cache | redesign | The purpose, one FS-wide table of existing snapshots that every shard can consult, is needed with snapshots; the rank-0 SnapServer, its full-table NOTIFY_PREP 2PC and the mds_snaptable encoding are replaced (D2). | Dead prepare_create_realm path and cephfs-table-tool compatibility are OUT. |
| [F-SNAP-003](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-003) | Deleted snapshots reported to the monitor (MRemoveSnaps) | contract | OSDs trim clones only after the monitor marks snap ids removed: the MDS must send MRemoveSnaps (pool -> snapid and destroy seq) and re-send until the monitor echoes it. | Pre-octopus removed_snaps handling is OUT. |
| [F-SNAP-004](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-004) | MDS data writes carry the realm SnapContext | contract | MDS-issued purge and truncate writes must carry the realm SnapContext, or post-snapshot data leaks into snapshots. | P0 sends an empty SnapContext but should plumb it through purge and truncate from day one; the uninline path is OUT. |
| [F-SNAP-010](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-010) | Global realm broadcast on every snap-table operation | redesign | Needed only so that hard-linked inodes in PARENT_GLOBAL realms learn of new snapshots (P1); C++ sends one message per session on every rank for every snapshot op. | With D3, hard links inside subvolumes never use the global realm; notify only sessions holding caps in global realms. |
| [F-SNAP-011](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-011) | Hard-link global realm (PARENT_GLOBAL) | redesign | Hard-linked inodes outside subvolumes need snapshot coverage from all their parents; relevant only with snapshots and only in the root shard (links never cross shards, D4). | Design now: P0 hard links in the root shard carry no global realm; P1 must convert them when snapshots arrive. |
| [F-SNAP-012](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-012) | Past-parent snapshots on rename and unlink | redesign | Keeps renamed or unlinked inodes covered by the snapshots of the realm they left (prior_parent_snaps in traces); P1 with snapshots. | The realm node carries past_parent_snaps from P0 (F-SNAP-006); per-shard strays must be able to keep such inodes (F-SNAP-028). |
| [F-SNAP-014](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-014) | Remove a snapshot (RMSNAP, rmdir in .snap) | contract | rmdir in .snap (mgr/volumes, snap_schedule pruning, cephfs-mirror); unreachable in P0 because no snapshot exists and clients look the name up first. | P0 answers ENOENT. |
| [F-SNAP-015](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-015) | Rename a snapshot (RENAMESNAP) | contract | RENAMESNAP (cephfs-mirror propagates snapshot renames); P1 with snapshots. | P0 answers ENOENT. |
| [F-SNAP-016](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-016) | Snapshot metadata at creation (SnapPayload) | contract | cephfs-mirror stores primary_snap_id as snapshot metadata at creation; P1 with MKSNAP. |  |
| [F-SNAP-017](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-017) | Modify snapshot metadata (SNAP_METADATA op) | contract | cephfs-mirror and the mgr mirroring module write sync checkpoints with SNAP_METADATA (CREATE, CREATE\|EXCL, REMOVE); P1 (D8 mirroring). | P0 answers ENOENT.<br>Risk: The mirror daemon holds only 'allow r' MDS caps and depends on SNAP_METADATA doing no MDS-cap write check; do not add one. |
| [F-SNAP-018](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-018) | allow_new_snaps filesystem flag | contract | fs set allow_new_snaps gates MKSNAP once snapshots exist; P0 behaves as if the flag were off. |  |
| [F-SNAP-019](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-019) | Per-directory snapshot limit (mds_max_snaps_per_dir) | contract | EMLINK at mds_max_snaps_per_dir; mgr/snap_schedule reads this MDS option to size retention. |  |
| [F-SNAP-023](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-023) | Read-only snapshot semantics and snapshot caps | contract | Snapshot inodes are read-only with read-only caps and snapflush-aware opens; P1. EROFS for mutations on snapshot paths is already P0 (F-NS-017). |  |
| [F-SNAP-024](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-024) | Snapshot attributes in InodeStat (snap_btime, snap_metadata) | contract | ceph.snap.btime (mgr/volumes snapshot info created_at) and snap_metadata (cephfs-mirror) come from InodeStat at a snapid; P1. | P0 encodes a zero snap_btime and empty snap_metadata. |
| [F-SNAP-025](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-025) | Snapshot inode lookup by ino (LOOKUPINO with snapid) | contract | Kernel NFS export of snapshot file handles; P1 with snapshots. | P0: LOOKUPINO with a snapid -> ESTALE. |
| [F-SNAP-026](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-026) | Metadata copy-on-write (first/last ranges, snapped dentries, old_inodes) | redesign | Snapshot views need dentry and inode versions as of each snapshot; P1, but the P0 metadata format must allow several versions per name and per inode. | Design now: versioned dentry keys ([first, last] with a head marker), first recorded on every dentry/inode from P0 (or defined as older than any snapshot), room for old_inodes and for snapped file inodes keyed (ino, last). |
| [F-SNAP-027](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-027) | Ancestor pre-COW and the COW sequence (global or subvolume-local) | redesign | Directory stats as of each snapshot must be preserved (mgr/volumes clone progress reads rbytes/rentries of snapshot directories); P1. | With shards a per-shard seq (C++ mds_use_global_snaprealm_seq_for_subvol=false behaviour) avoids FS-wide coupling. |
| [F-SNAP-028](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-028) | Lazy trimming of deleted-snapshot metadata | redesign | Metadata of deleted snapshots must eventually be removed and strays with snapshot history kept; P1. |  |
| [F-SNAP-029](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-029) | Directory snapshot diff (READDIR_SNAPDIFF) | contract | cephfs-mirror incremental sync (D8: snapdiff and mirroring are P1). | P0 answers EOPNOTSUPP. |
| [F-SNAP-030](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-030) | File block diff between snapshots (FILE_BLOCKDIFF) | contract | cephfs-mirror block-level sync (D8: P1); do not advertise CEPHFS_FEATURE_BLOCKDIFF (23) before then, so libcephfs returns EOPNOTSUPP itself. | Risk: C++ asserts on swapped snapshot arguments (client input); return an error instead. |
| [F-SNAP-053](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-053) | Snapshots only at the subvolume root | contract | Restricts MKSNAP below a subvolume root (EPERM); meaningful only once MKSNAP exists (P0 refuses all, F-SNAP-013). |  |
| [F-SNAP-055](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-055) | Snapshot visibility flag (ceph.dir.subvolume.snaps.visible) | contract | Advisory flag for hiding .snap (libcephfs with client_respect_subvolume_snapshot_visibility, default false), used by mgr/volumes snapshot_visibility set/get; meaningful only with snapshots. | Cheap to store in the P0 realm node; reaches clients only with bit 19. |
| [F-ADM-097](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-097) | mgr/volumes: subvolume snapshots | contract | Snapshots are P1 (D8); in P0 MKSNAP returns EPERM (as with allow_new_snaps off), so `fs subvolume snapshot create` fails cleanly and ls is empty. |  |
| [F-ADM-098](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-098) | mgr/volumes: snapshot visibility | contract | Controls .snap visibility per subvolume; only meaningful with snapshots (D8). | Risk: In P0 accept ceph.dir.subvolume.snaps.visible on realm roots (store or C++-style no-op) so the command does not fail. |
| [F-ADM-099](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-099) | mgr/volumes: snapshot clone | contract | Clones copy from snapshots (D8: P1) and need readable snapshot data, rstat of snapshot inodes, symlink st_size/ctime ordering and the _index/clone symlinks. |  |
| [F-ADM-103](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-103) | mgr/snap_schedule: scheduled snapshots and retention | contract | Scheduled snapshots are P1 (D8); the module stores its SQLite DB in the metadata pool and reads mds_max_snaps_per_dir. | Legacy snap_db_v0 import is out.<br>Risk: The MDS must never touch snap_db_v0.db* objects in the metadata pool (F-STORE-102). |
| [F-ADM-104](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-104) | mgr/mirroring: enable, peers, bootstrap and directory distribution | contract | Snapshot mirroring is P1 (D8); needs ceph.mirror.info on the remote root (raw xattrs, XATTR_CREATE -> EEXIST) and coexistence with the cephfs_mirror objects in the metadata pool. |  |
| [F-ADM-105](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-105) | mgr/mirroring: checkpoints through snapshot metadata | contract | Mirror checkpoints use SNAP_METADATA on snapshots (P1, D8), which must stay caps-unchecked except for the uid range. |  |
| [F-ADM-117](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-117) | cephfs-mirror daemon | contract | Snapshot mirroring is P1 (D8): snapdiff, blockdiff (feature bit 23), SNAP_METADATA under `allow r`, flock LOCK_EX\|LOCK_NB on remote dir roots, the ceph.mirror.dirty_snap_id plain xattr and sync_fs. |  |

| Entry | Name | Why |

| [F-STORE-078](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-078) | Table server/client two-phase commit and its journal events | The table 2PC is MDS-to-MDS (free); P1 snapshots can use a file-system-wide snapid allocator plus shard-local realm updates (F-STORE-077). |
| [F-STORE-079](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-079) | Table server placement on rank 0 | MDSMap tableserver is produced by the mon and ignored by the kernel; placement of a P1 snapid service is free. |
| [F-STORE-080](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-080) | NOTIFY_PREP fan-out barrier | Full-table fan-out to every rank is part of the C++ table protocol (MDS-to-MDS, free). |
| [F-STORE-081](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-081) | Server-local table updates and table queries | C++ table-server internals; tracking snapids still to purge and MRemoveSnaps come with P1 snapshots (F-STORE-077). |
| [F-STORE-082](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-082) | Table 2PC recovery and message delivery rules | Recovery rules of the C++ table 2PC (MDS-to-MDS). |
| [F-STORE-083](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-083) | Resolve integration of table commits | Resolve-time exchange of the C++ table 2PC (MDS-to-MDS). |
| [F-SNAP-005](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-005) | allow_multimds_snaps flag and MULTIMDS_WITH_OLDSNAPS health check (legacy) | Legacy (old-data): guards pre-Mimic snapshots; new file systems start with ALLOW_MULTIMDS_SNAPS and never reach it. |

## C++ internals

### C++ internals: OUT (137)

| Entry | Name | Why |
|---|---|---|
| [F-CLU-079](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-079) | Post-resolve cache cleanup and authority bits | Orphan trimming and auth-bit recomputation after resolve are C++ cache mechanics for shared subtrees; nothing is client-visible. |
| [F-CLU-091](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-091) | Scatterlock recovery and NEED_RECOVER | Scatterlocks and NEED_RECOVER are C++ cross-rank lock machinery (D16); inside a shard dirstat/rstat are transactional. |
| [F-CLU-093](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-093) | Rejoin cache repair: invented objects, stale fragmentation, missed link/unlink | Invented placeholder objects, stale fragtree mapping and missed link/unlink repair exist only for C++ replicas and dirfrags (D5). |
| [F-CLU-111](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-111) | Global mds_lock (FIFO fair mutex) | The global mds_lock is the C++ serialization the project removes (constraint 5, D16). |
| [F-CLU-112](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-112) | Completion contexts and the rank finisher | MDSContext completions and the rank finisher are C++ re-entry mechanics (D16); their visible effects (restart on -EBLOCKLISTED, ordered journal completions) are covered by F-CLU-105 and the WAL design. |
| [F-CLU-113](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-113) | Progress thread and rank waiter queues | The progress thread and waiter queues are C++ internals (D16); the waits they implement (map epoch, active state, recovery) are kept as behaviours in their own entries. |
| [F-CLU-117](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-117) | Tick and the shared timer | A single 5 s tick under mds_lock is a C++ mechanism; each periodic duty (session liveness, reconnect timeout, health refresh, stopping progress) is classified in its own entry. |
| [F-CLU-118](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-118) | Thread inventory of the daemon and its subsystems | Thread inventory of the C++ process; the Rust threading model is free. |
| [F-CLU-119](../mds-rust-map/features/01-cluster-membership-and-ha.md#f-clu-119) | MDS-originated client requests (MDSMetaRequest) | MDS-to-MDS client requests serve cross-rank stray reintegration and quota-realm creation; with per-shard strays and realms created by the owning shard (D3) nothing crosses ranks. |
| [F-CAPS-049](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-049) | Lock cache attachment | Request-locking fast path of the C++ lock cache (D16); a P1 Dc/Dr design (F-CAPS-048) defines its own. Never exercised today (leases 8 G1). |
| [F-CAPS-061](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-061) | Per-object lock catalogue | C++ lock partition and per-object order (D16); clients see only the four cap groups (F-CAPS-001) and the new engine chooses its own granularity. Dead-code legacy parts out. |
| [F-CAPS-062](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-062) | SimpleLock state machine (sm_simplelock) | C++ state machine (D16); its client-visible outcomes (shared caps when readable, exclusive caps for one client, s revoked before a change) are kept by F-CAPS-004/006/067. Dead-code legacy part out. |
| [F-CAPS-063](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-063) | ScatterLock state machine (nestlock, dirfragtreelock) | Scatterlocks exist because one directory's stats are updated on several ranks; directories are not split across ranks (D5) and cross-shard rstat aggregation is a new mechanism (D4). Dead TSYN family out. |
| [F-CAPS-064](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-064) | FileLock state machine (sm_filelock) | C++ state machine (D16); the F-cap sets per sharing mode are kept by F-CAPS-004/006 and size recovery by F-CAPS-022. |
| [F-CAPS-066](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-066) | Local locks (versionlock, quiescelock) | versionlock pipelining and the quiescelock are C++ mechanisms (D16); quiesce itself is P2 (QOS). |
| [F-CAPS-068](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-068) | Lock-mode grant rules (rdlock / wrlock / xlock compatibility) | Superseded by the S/X/IS/IX lock manager (D16); the POSIX atomicity it serves is owned by the namespace entries. |
| [F-CAPS-069](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-069) | Lock-request-driven state changes and journal nudging | Lock kicking is internal (D16), and with commit before reply plus group commit (D7) there are no early-replied lock holders to nudge. |
| [F-CAPS-070](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-070) | Scatter data exchange and writebehind | Multi-rank dirstat/rstat gathering inside one directory disappears (D5); cross-shard rstat aggregation is a new mechanism (D4, tenancy). |
| [F-CAPS-071](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-071) | Scatter nudging | Same as F-CAPS-070; rstat freshness toward clients is owned by tenancy entries (F-SNAP-036). |
| [F-CAPS-072](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-072) | Inter-MDS lock protocol (MLock) | MDS-to-MDS only; without replicated lock states there is nothing to synchronise. Dead-code legacy part out. |
| [F-CAPS-073](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-073) | Replica-initiated lock state requests | Replica lock requests disappear with whole-shard ownership (D5, D16). |
| [F-CAPS-074](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-074) | Lock state across replication, migration and rejoin | Lock states never cross ranks in the new design; shard migration ships cap and file-lock state instead (F-MULTI-006). |
| [F-CAPS-075](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-075) | acquire_locks and lock vectors | Superseded by the transaction engine's plan and acquire steps (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-076](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-076) | Per-object lock order | C++ deadlock-avoidance convention (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-077](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-077) | Cross-object ordering rules and no upgrades | C++ deadlock-avoidance rules (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-078](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-078) | Ancestor snaplock/policylock try-rdlocks and layout inheritance | Mechanism (D16); intention locks on ancestors in the new engine serialise policy changes (layout, quota, snapshots) against operations below. Layout inheritance itself is owned by namespace and tenancy entries. |
| [F-CAPS-079](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-079) | Path traversal locking and path helpers | C++ path-locking helpers (D16); path resolution and routing are owned by namespace and cache entries. |
| [F-CAPS-080](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-080) | Implicit versionlock (pipelining and peer rollback) | Projection pipelining and peer rollback guard of the C++ MDS (D16, no cross-rank 2PC in P0 per D4). |
| [F-CAPS-082](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-082) | Local auth pins on locked objects | Auth pins and freezing are C++ internals (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-083](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-083) | Remote auth pins and the rename frozen auth pin | Remote auth pins serve cross-rank operations; no cross-rank namespace transactions in P0 (D4). |
| [F-CAPS-084](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-084) | Remote xlocks and remote wrlocks | Remote xlocks and wrlocks serve cross-rank link, unlink and rename (D4: EXDEV across shards). |
| [F-CAPS-085](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-085) | Remote lock teardown and peer failure | Teardown of cross-rank lock state (D4, D16). |
| [F-CAPS-087](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-087) | FIFO lock waiters with synchronous wake-up | C++ waiter queues and synchronous wake-ups (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-088](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-088) | Non-blocking lock helpers | C++ lock helpers (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-089](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-089) | Freeze versus auth-pin deadlock breaker | Freeze versus auth-pin deadlock is a C++ freezing artefact (D16). |
| [F-CAPS-090](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-090) | Lock waits on a degraded cluster | Waits for replicated lock recovery disappear with whole-shard ownership and per-shard recovery (D5, D16). |
| [F-CAPS-091](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-091) | xlock lifecycle | C++ xlock mechanics (D16); the creator's immediate exclusive caps are kept by F-CAPS-010. |
| [F-CAPS-094](../mds-rust-map/features/03-caps-leases-and-locking.md#f-caps-094) | Lock release at safe reply and cleanup | C++ lock release order (D16). |

| [F-NS-006](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-006) | GETATTR/LOOKUP batching (BatchOp) | Coalescing of identical GETATTR/LOOKUP under mds_lock; superseded by concurrent lock-free reads. No peer depends on it. |

Client-sent internal op codes get EOPNOTSUPP (F-NS-001). |
| [F-NS-009](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-009) | Request-path fault injection and test kill points | QA kill points and delays tied to C++ code positions, used only by qa suites. The Rust MDS needs its own failpoints for crash-recovery testing, which is test infrastructure, not a catalog feature. |
| [F-NS-052](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-052) | MDS-originated RENAME (stray reintegration and migration) | MDS-to-MDS rename messages exist because strays are per rank; with per-shard strays (D3) and no cross-shard links (D4) reintegration is a local transaction (purpose owned by F-BG-011), and stray migration is replaced by whole-shard handoff (D6). |
| [F-NS-054](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-054) | Peer requests and the LINK/UNLINK two-phase commit | Cross-rank LINK/UNLINK 2PC is unnecessary: a shard lives on one rank and cross-shard links get EXDEV (D4). |
| [F-NS-055](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-055) | RMDIR witness protocol | Witnesses exist for dirfrag subtrees spread across ranks; D5 gives one frag per directory and a shard lives on one rank. Removing a shard root uses the D4 mechanism. |
| [F-NS-056](../mds-rust-map/features/04-namespace-and-posix-operations.md#f-ns-056) | RENAME cross-rank two-phase commit | No general cross-rank 2PC (D4): renames across shards get EXDEV; shard-root moves in the root shard use a dedicated mechanism. |
| [F-CACHE-012](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-012) | Inode projection (read-copy-update) | Projection chains are C++ concurrency internals (D16); the transaction engine provides isolation and P0 replies show only committed state (D7). |
| [F-CACHE-013](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-013) | Dirfrag version sequence and fnode projection | Dirfrag version sequences and fnode projection are C++ commit-tracking internals (D2, D16). |
| [F-CACHE-014](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-014) | Projected dentry linkage (primary, remote, null) | Linkage projection is internal (D16); the needed linkage kinds (primary, hard link) are persisted per F-STORE-049/050 and alternate_name storage comes with fscrypt/charmap (P2). |
| [F-CACHE-015](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-015) | Dirty tracking on journal segments | Per-segment dirty lists are C++ write-back bookkeeping, replaced by per-shard checkpointing (F-STORE-016). |
| [F-CACHE-020](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-020) | Underwater item reconciliation after replay | Reconciling replayed dirty items with already written objects is an artifact of C++ lazy write-back. |
| [F-CACHE-024](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-024) | Bloom filter of trimmed names | Optimization of negative lookups after trimming, with no external contract. |
| [F-CACHE-025](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-025) | Null dentries | In-cache representation of negative and pending-delete entries; negative-dentry leases to clients belong to the lease entries (caps category). |
| [F-CACHE-026](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-026) | Reference counts and upward pinning | Pin and refcount machinery is C++ internal (D16); the outcome it serves (purge an unlinked file only after its last cap and request) belongs to the stray and purge entries. |
| [F-CACHE-027](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-027) | Inode maps and object registry | In-memory indexes of the C++ cache, with no external contract. |
| [F-CACHE-028](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-028) | Auth pins with one-level nested accounting | Auth pins are named in D16 as C++ internal. |
| [F-CACHE-029](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-029) | Dirfrag freeze (freeze_dir) | Used only by directory fragmentation, which D5 removes; freezing is internal (D16). |
| [F-CACHE-030](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-030) | Subtree freeze (freeze_tree) | Subtree freezing is a C++ mechanism (D16, like F-MULTI-003); the needed whole-shard drain at handoff (block, then forward) is carried by F-MULTI-018 and the handoff by F-MULTI-001/021 and F-STORE-033. |
| [F-CACHE-032](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-032) | Single-inode freeze, ambiguous inode auth and frozen-inode tracking | Serves only cross-rank rename (EXDEV across shards, D4) and lock-cache bookkeeping; internal (D16). |
| [F-CACHE-033](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-033) | Waiter queues on cache objects | Waiter queues are C++ internal (D16). |
| [F-CACHE-034](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-034) | Waiter completion semantics | Continuation completion semantics are C++ internal (D16). |
| [F-CACHE-037](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-037) | Subtree create, merge and bounded-adjust primitives | Dynamic subtree splitting and merging is replaced by whole-shard ownership; shard roots come from ceph.dir.subvolume (D3). |
| [F-CACHE-038](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-038) | Ambiguous authority and WAIT_SINGLEAUTH | Ambiguous dir_auth states belong to C++ migration and 2PC; a shard has one owner per placement epoch and requests during handoff wait or are forwarded (F-CACHE-036, F-MULTI-018). |
| [F-CACHE-039](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-039) | Subtree map maintenance on directory rename | Cross-shard renames return EXDEV (D4) and shard ownership does not depend on paths, so renames never change the shard map. |
| [F-CACHE-040](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-040) | Aux subtrees | Aux subtrees implement C++ export pins; ceph.dir.pin becomes a whole-shard placement hint (D6). |
| [F-CACHE-048](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-048) | open_ino peer protocol | MDS-to-MDS inode search (free, D2); replaced by the inode-number index (F-CACHE-047). |
| [F-CACHE-049](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-049) | Batched dentry-key fetches for open_ino | C++ batching of backtrace-driven dentry fetches during rejoin. |
| [F-CACHE-051](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-051) | Foreign mdsdir and stray base resolution | Per-rank ~mdsN and stray directories are not exposed, and cross-rank stray operations disappear with per-shard strays (D3). |
| [F-CACHE-052](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-052) | Replica map and nonces | Replica bookkeeping of the C++ MDS-to-MDS protocol; no general replicas (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-053](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-053) | Discover protocol (pull replication) | Pull replication between ranks is removed by D5. |
| [F-CACHE-054](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-054) | Replica encodings | MDS-to-MDS encodings are free (D2, D5). |
| [F-CACHE-056](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-056) | Push link/unlink to replicas | Dentry replicas do not exist (D5) and MDS-to-MDS messages are free. |
| [F-CACHE-057](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-057) | Replica refresh through lock-state payloads | Lock-state payloads are C++ internals (D16); the needed refresh of shard-root copies is part of F-CACHE-055. |
| [F-CACHE-058](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-058) | Replica expiry (MCacheExpire) | Replica expiry protocol, removed with replicas (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-059](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-059) | Dentry LRUs (lru and bottom_lru) | LRU policy is internal; the memory-budget contract is F-CACHE-061. |
| [F-CACHE-062](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-062) | Trim throttle | Trim-throttle tuning of the C++ LRU. |
| [F-CACHE-063](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-063) | Trim pass and per-object removal | C++ trim-pass mechanics. |
| [F-CACHE-064](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-064) | Upkeep thread | C++ upkeep thread; the duties it drives (lease expiry, cap recall, memory sampling) belong to their own entries. |
| [F-CACHE-065](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-065) | Heap release to the OS | tcmalloc-specific heap release. |
| [F-CACHE-066](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-066) | Empty-subtree trimming and empty-import return | Empty-subtree and empty-import handling of the C++ subtree map. |
| [F-CACHE-067](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-067) | Expiry of a stopping peer's mdsdir replicas | No replicas of another rank's mdsdir exist; a stopping rank hands off its shards instead (D6). |
| [F-CACHE-068](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-068) | Recovery-time non-auth trimming | Non-auth cache cleanup for C++ resolve and rejoin; no replicas (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-082](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-082) | Size-triggered split and split checks | Directory fragmentation is removed by D5: no size-triggered splits. |
| [F-CACHE-083](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-083) | Fast split | No fast split: fragmentation is removed by D5. |
| [F-CACHE-084](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-084) | Temperature-triggered split | No temperature-triggered splits or popularity counters (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-085](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-085) | Size-triggered merge | No merges: fragmentation is removed by D5. |
| [F-CACHE-086](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-086) | Fragmentation preconditions and exclusions | No fragmentation to gate (D5); the legacy /.ceph exclusion is out too. |
| [F-CACHE-087](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-087) | Fragment operation (FRAGMENTDIR) | No FRAGMENTDIR operation (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-088](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-088) | Dentry repartitioning and stat-delta preservation | No in-memory refragmentation (D5); internal partitioning of the new store keeps exact stats by its own means. |
| [F-CACHE-089](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-089) | New dirfrag object write and old object purge | No dirfrag object rewrite (D5); dirfrag objects are C++ format (D2). |
| [F-CACHE-090](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-090) | Replica notification of refragmentation | No refragmentation notifies; MDS-to-MDS messages are free (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-091](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-091) | Fragtree propagation through dirfragtreelock | No fragtree changes and no scatterlocks (D5, D16). |
| [F-CACHE-092](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-092) | Subtree map maintenance on split and merge | No subtree-map edits on split or merge (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-093](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-093) | Abort, requeue and cancellation of fragment operations | No fragment operations to abort or requeue (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-094](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-094) | Recovery of in-flight fragment operations | No in-flight fragment operations to recover (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-095](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-095) | Forced refragmentation during replay, resolve and rejoin | Replay never refragments (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-096](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-096) | Migration and fragmentation mutual exclusion | Nothing to exclude: shard migration has no fragmentation to race with (D5). |
| [F-CACHE-097](../mds-rust-map/features/05-metadata-cache-and-dirfrags.md#f-cache-097) | Effects of a fragment freeze on clients | No fragment freezes (D5); client stalls during shard handoff belong to the migration entries. |
| [F-STORE-011](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-011) | Ordered completions and the safe position | Ordered completions on one finisher and safe_pos are C++ internals; the WAL redesign (F-STORE-007) must only acknowledge records that are completely durable. |
| [F-STORE-034](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-034) | EFragment journal protocol and old dirfrag object cleanup | Directory fragmentation is removed (D5) and EFragment is C++ journal format (D2). |
| [F-STORE-112](../mds-rust-map/features/06-journal-and-storage.md#f-store-112) | Developer and fault-injection options | C++ dev and fault-injection options used by QA; the new MDS needs its own test hooks. |
| [F-MULTI-003](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-003) | Subtree freeze and auth-pin drain | Freezing and auth pins are C++ internals (D16); the client-visible contract (requests block without error, then are forwarded) is kept by F-MULTI-018 through a shard-level quiesce. |
| [F-MULTI-004](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-004) | Lock set held during migration | Scatter-gather locks exist because another rank sums the dirfrag's stats (D5, D16). |
| [F-MULTI-005](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-005) | Prep phase: spanning-tree replication and bound opening | Spanning-tree replicas and bound dirfrags do not exist for self-contained shards (D3, D5). |
| [F-MULTI-007](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-007) | Bystander warning and notify | No third rank replicates shard contents (D5); other ranks learn the new owner from the shard map. |
| [F-MULTI-008](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-008) | Cache-expire routing during migration | Cache expires exist only for cross-rank replicas (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-009](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-009) | Empty import return | Empty-import bounce is an artefact of dirfrag subtrees; a shard is placed whole. |
| [F-MULTI-011](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-011) | Export splitting of large subtrees | Shards are indivisible placement units (D3); no export splitting. |
| [F-MULTI-024](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-024) | Freeze deadlock breaker (mds_freeze_tree_timeout) | Export side of the freeze deadlock breaker (F-CAPS-089), a C++ freezing artefact (D16). |
| [F-MULTI-026](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-026) | Decayed popularity counters (DecayCounter, meta_load) | Popularity counters feed only the default balancer, which is out (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-027](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-027) | Popularity hits on metadata operations | Same as F-MULTI-026 (D5); O(depth) counter updates per operation also disappear. |
| [F-MULTI-028](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-028) | Popularity transfer on export, import, subtree change, rename and split | Same as F-MULTI-026 (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-029](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-029) | Per-rank load (mds_load_t) and mds_bal_mode | Balancer load metric (D5); the load_cent and q gauges have no known consumer. |
| [F-MULTI-031](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-031) | Heartbeat epoch exchange (MHeartbeat) | MDS-to-MDS heartbeat of the default balancer (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-032](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-032) | Default rebalance target computation (prep_rebalance) | Default balancer planning (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-033](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-033) | Export selection (try_rebalance, find_exports) | Default balancer export selection (D5). |
| [F-MULTI-034](../mds-rust-map/features/07-multi-mds.md#f-multi-034) | Idle import return (mds_bal_idle_threshold) | Balancer-epoch behaviour (D5), off by default. |
| [F-SNAP-036](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-036) | Deferred stat propagation (dirty rstat, updated scatterlocks, tick and nudge) | Dirty-rstat lists, scatter_tick/nudge and LOCK_AC_NUDGE are C++ scatterlock machinery (D16); the bounded-lag requirement is carried by F-SNAP-035 (in shard) and F-SNAP-037 (across shards). |
| [F-SNAP-038](../mds-rust-map/features/08-snapshots-quotas-subvolumes.md#f-snap-038) | Journal segment expiry waits for stat folding | Couples rank-journal segment trimming to scatterlock folding; with per-shard WALs and no scatterlocks the generic WAL checkpoint invariant covers it. |
| [F-BG-003](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-003) | Stray directory rotation per log segment and pre-fragmentation | Rotation per log segment and pre-splitting exist only to spread one shared per-rank stray namespace over dirfrags; per-shard stray storage has no client-visible dirfrags (D3, D5). |
| [F-BG-005](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-005) | Stray dentry replication to other ranks | Strays and every cap on a shard's inodes live on the shard's owning rank (D3, D5), so stray replicas and cap export to the auth disappear. |
| [F-BG-012](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-012) | Stray migration between ranks | Hard links stay inside one shard (D4), a shard lives on one rank and rank stop hands off whole shards (D6), so strays never migrate between ranks. |
| [F-BG-018](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-018) | Parallel execution with contiguous expire_pos progress | Contiguous expire_pos tracking is how the C++ Journaler-based queue persists progress; durable progress is part of the per-shard queue (F-BG-013, F-BG-019). |
| [F-BG-034](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-034) | Scrub stamps on inodes and dirfrags | Lazily persisted scrub stamps in inode_t/fnode_t are C++ format fields that no peer reads; an incremental scrub (F-BG-033) would define its own. |
| [F-BG-041](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-041) | Journal trim after repairs | Forcing repaired metadata out of the C++ MDLog is journal-specific; repairs in the new design are durable at WAL commit (D7). |
| [F-BG-043](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-043) | Base inode on-disk validation (root, ~mdsN) | Validates C++ base-inode objects (<ino>.00000000.inode, ~mdsN) that the new format does not have; failures were only logged. |
| [F-BG-045](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-045) | Dirfrag fragstat/rstat validation and repair | Dirfrag-level fragstat/rstat is C++ structure (D5); the stat check and repair live in F-BG-044. |
| [F-BG-046](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-046) | Dentry snapshot-bound check during scrub | Checks the C++ dentry first/last snapid encoding (the case first-damage.py targets, OUT by D2); the P1 snapshot design brings its own invariants. |
| [F-BG-054](../mds-rust-map/features/09-background-services.md#f-bg-054) | Dentry snapshot-bound corruption check at load and commit | Guards the C++ dentry first/last snapid encoding against a historical corruption (first-damage.py, OUT by D2); generic load-time validation is F-BG-053 and snapshot invariants come with P1 snapshots (D8). |
| [F-QOS-005](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-005) | QoS scheduler threads and hand-off to request processing | Three scheduler threads per rank and a hand-off under mds_lock are C++ internals (D16). |
| [F-QOS-009](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-009) | QoS bucket state lifecycle tied to sessions | Per-rank in-memory buckets created and destroyed with sessions are dmClock integration internals; per-shard scheduling state belongs to the shard. |
| [F-QOS-010](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-010) | QoS enable, disable and drain on rank state and option changes | Enable, disable and drain of the dmClock queue on rank-state changes are integration internals (with a self-deadlock); a per-shard scheduler is always on and moves with its shard. |
| [F-QOS-047](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-047) | Quiescing inodes that appear during a quiesce | A subtree-level quiesce lock covers inodes loaded or imported later by construction, so no per-inode follow-up ops are needed. |
| [F-QOS-048](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-048) | Quiesce and directory fragmentation | There is no client-visible directory fragmentation to coordinate with (D5). |
| [F-QOS-051](../mds-rust-map/features/10-qos-metrics-quiesce-quarantine.md#f-qos-051) | Quiesce dispatch throttle and heartbeat resets | Dispatch throttling and heartbeat resets exist because C++ quiesces inode by inode under mds_lock. |
| [F-ADM-063](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-063) | Option apply-class anomalies | C++ defects (misspelled tracked keys, values ignored at startup, unused options); nothing to replicate. |
| [F-ADM-065](../mds-rust-map/features/11-security-admin-and-observability.md#f-adm-065) | Fault injection, killpoints and debug options | C++ dev options that crash or corrupt C++ internals for qa; the Rust MDS needs its own failpoint framework. |
